## N. PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED

Settled by passes 1-3 (`panel_20260903_121558`, `panel_20260903_152458`, and the measurement
artifacts under `regress/artifacts/pcc2widthkills/`), warrants inline:

1. **The width is LEGAL** -- `mvme_pcctworeg.h`: "bytes, two-bytes, or four-bytes".
2. **`memory_readmax64` composes BIG-ENDIAN**: `(uint8_t) idata' is the HIGHEST-address lane;
   a bare deletion stores the neighbour's byte.  MEASURED (`M_DELETE' -> `rb genctl' 0xbb).
3. **Reachability:** every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked before memory_rw
   (`#ifndef LS_1 ... m88k_exception(MISALIGNED)' then return) and `relative_addr %= 0x40'
   keeps parity.  SIX even blocks (GENCTL 0x02, TxCTL 0x16, TxICR 0x1a, SCCERR 0x1c, SCCTX
   0x1e, IPL 0x3e) are GUEST-reachable; EIGHT odd OFFSETS -- the six odd-only blocks VECBASE
   0x03, PSCALEADJ 0x15, SCCICR 0x1d, SCCRX 0x1f, SCSIICR 0x2f, MASK 0x3f, plus the T1CTL 0x17
   / T1ICR 0x1b labels of the shared TxCTL/TxICR blocks -- are OPERATOR-only.  Byte accesses
   (`st.b', `put b') are never alignment-checked.
4. **The operator path reaches the SAME guard**: `put h' warns, then `store_16bit_word' ->
   ONE `memory_rw(len 2)' -> the same arm.  `put w' / `put d' likewise give len 4 / 8.
   **A debugger READ does not reach the arm**: `memory_rw' dispatches a device only if
   `(!no_exceptions || (flags & DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))' and pcc2 registers
   `DM_DEFAULT' -- so the six odd blocks' READ-side cells are unreachable by construction.
5. **Arm bodies** (inlined below): VECBASE stores `idata & 0xf0' (+ HUH print); SCCICR
   `& 0x1f'; SCCRX and SCSIICR `& 0xdf'; each of those four then (except VECBASE) calls
   `reassert_interrupts()'.  PSCALEADJ stores nothing, prints "ignored (value ...)".  IPL's
   write body stores nothing and prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per
   process (#119 static).  SCCERR's write body is `= 0' and nothing in the model stores a
   nonzero SCCERR.  TxCTL `& 3'; TxICR `&= ~0x17 |= idata & 0x17'.  GENCTL and MASK store the
   byte and reassert.  SCCRXIACK has NO width guard and never had one: its body ignores idata.
6. **`fatal()' returns**; the announce is once per block per instance via `d->warned', a
   32-bit field with twelve bits assigned, written ONLY inside `pcc2_width_decline()'.
   `reassert_interrupts()' exits the host only on `GENCTL & 0x04' (pcc2c040kill, filed);
   every GENCTL value in the probe keeps that bit clear.  Every probe session is a fresh
   `pty.fork'/`execv' of the emulator.
7. **The ruling, applied to all twelve now**: clause (d) is a FLOOR; a disclosed asymmetry
   cannot discharge it.  Announce-then-fall-through leaves every `dcl' row and `latch all'
   green by construction, so its only channel is a readback (storing arms) or the arm's own
   print (print-only arms).
8. **Pass-3 and pass-4 rosters**: eight answered each time (codex, kimi, grok, glm, deepseek,
   minimax, opus, fable5); agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (reserved for measurement).  Pass 4's
   Opus reader measured; its fable5 seat ratified on an analytic sweep that omitted the helper
   edits and was overturned by that measurement.
9. **The debugger's `put b' never reaches the arms** (memory_rw with NO_EXCEPTIONS is not
   dispatched to a DM_DEFAULT device); `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do; guest
   `st.b' reaches every offset.  MEASURED (part 3: a grown guard escaped the `put b' row twice
   with the mutation proven; part 3c: killed by the guest-store form).

process-global static.  The
tree's own header says PCC2 registers "can be accessed as bytes, two-bytes, or

    READS   ACCEPT -- the memcpy above the switch already served them, raw bytes in
            address order, width-correct by construction.  The guard discarded a
            correct answer.
    WRITES  DECLINE + per-instance latched announce -- every write arm uses `idata',
            the big-endian-composed value, which is "the addressed byte" ONLY at
            len==1.  Deleting the guard would store the NEIGHBOUR's lane.

    * an accidental per-lane compose (GENCTL would read 0xAA);
    * the value a wide READ composes -- lane order and width -- not merely survival;

      lands and reads back through the arm's own mask with NO decline message; at all

static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
{
	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
	}
}

	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:

			break;

			if (idata & ~0xf0)

		    the WIDTH latch; the body's own #119 `static int w' below stays
		    process-global (pcc2pscaleflood's family; not this round's).  */
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_IPL_LEN, "PCCTWO_IPL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			static int w = 0;	/* #119: was exit(1) (IPL is read-only) */

			    "ignored ]\atic int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above -- that is
		    the WIDTH latch; the body's own #119 `static int w' below stays
		    process-global (pcc2pscaleflood's family; not this round's).  */

			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_MASK_LEN, "PCCTWO_MASK", len);
			break;

	case PCCTWO_T2ICR:
	case PCCTWO_T1ICR:

## PRETABLE_amendments.out (5-7), PRETABLE_pass4.out (8), PRETABLE_pass5.out (9-10)

```
PRETABLE AMENDMENTS -- pcc2widthkills, killtable2_20260903.  Split out of PRETABLE.out on pass 3.


SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.

Rows added (part 2 measures them):
  rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
                                      M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
  op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
                                      "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
                                      M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.


SECTION 6 -- PASS 3: THE CLASS CLOSURE.  Written after pass 3's seats landed and BEFORE
part 3 of the table.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Pass 3 found the SAME class a third time: the fable5 seat MEASURED announce-then-fall-through
at VECBASE as 82/82 (its `pcc2p3_fable/probe_mfall_vecbase.out`).  `rb vecbase' is GENCTL's
neighbour-lane check (st.b VECBASE; st.h GENCTL; ld.bu VECBASE) and Section 2 above credited
it as VECBASE's own readback -- a wrong record that hid the cell.  Independently, glm and grok
named IPL: its write body prints "write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; ignored" once per process and
stores nothing, so a fall-through has a MESSAGE channel exactly as PSCALEADJ does -- and the
fable5 seat had called that cell a no-op, the same direction the builder was wrong about
PSCALEADJ.  SCCERR's fall-through cell is EQUIVALENT BY CONSTRUCTION: the arm stores 0 and no
code in the model stores a nonzero SCCERR (grep), so decline and land are the same state.

The enumeration (12 blocks x 6 shapes) is now written into the probe's CONTRACT with a status
per cell; the two open cells get rows:
  rb vecbase own   st.b 0x50 at VECBASE; put h 0xaab8 at VECBASE; ld.bu -> 0x50.
                   M_FALL_VECBASE lands 0xb0 (0xaab8 & 0xf0) and also prints HUH.
  hwr ipl noig     st.h 0x0303 at IPL (guest-reachable); count of the read-only print == 0.
                   M_FALL_IPL prints it once.
The codex seat added two shapes the six-shape list did not have, both closable at all twelve
blocks by one cardinality row each:
  latch all w      `len != 1' -> `len == 2' (LEN's mirror) declines every halfword row and lets
                   a WORD land; twelve `put w' writes, twelve announces.  M_LEN2_VECBASE -> 11.
  acc all (+lv)    the contract claimed byte writes land "at every reachable arm" while acc
                   rows existed at five blocks.  Twelve legal `put b' writes, ZERO declines.
                   M_GROW_PSCALEADJ (`len != 1' -> `len != 0' at an arm with no acc row) -> 1.
The Opus reader seat MEASURED a ninth shape, 82/82, ONE character: `writeflag == MEM_WRITE'
-> `<= MEM_WRITE', the direction split's own conjunct nulled, so wide READS are declined,
announced as writes, skip the arm's read body and consume the latch bit.  No session holding
a wide read carried a decline-count row.  Closed with ZERO new sessions:
  hrd <block> nod  (six even blocks) and wrd sccerr nod: t.count(DECL) == 0 on the existing
                   wide-read sessions.  M_RDDECL_ALL (all twelve conjuncts) -> seven reds.
  The six odd blocks' read-side cells are UNREACHABLE by construction (guest wide reads at
  odd offsets alignment-fault before memory_rw; debugger reads do not dispatch to DM_DEFAULT
  devices) and are disclosed in the contract.
The T1 labels (deepseek): TxCTL/TxICR are SHARED blocks whose readbacks sat at the T2 offsets;
`rb t1ctl' / `rb t1icr' (operator path, own readbacks) close the T1 half.  M_FALL_TXCTL
(break removed at the shared block) -> rb t2ctl AND rb t1ctl both red.  SCCRXIACK (glm): the
thirteenth arm, never width-guarded, body ignores idata -- named in the contract as excluded.
Part 3 measures BASE 96/96, M_FALL_VECBASE, M_FALL_IPL, M_LEN2_VECBASE, M_GROW_PSCALEADJ,

the mutation PROVEN in the source (1 x `len != 0') and the binary newer than the source (3b's
proof lines).  So the row could not see the arm: the debugger's `put b' calls memory_rw with
NO_EXCEPTIONS, and memory_rw.c's device dispatch is `if (!no_exceptions || (flags &
DM_READS_HAVE_NO_SIDE_EFFECTS))'; pcc2 registers DM_DEFAULT, so a NO_EXCEPTIONS byte write is
never dispatched.  `put h/w/d' go through store_Nbit_word and do reach the arms (every other
cardinality row worked).  THE ROW WAS VACUOUS BY CONSTRUCTION -- the CHANNEL rule's failure
condition, caught by the table rather than at writing time.  `acc all' is now twelve GUEST
`st.b' stores (legal at every offset), and part 3c re-measures BASE + M_GROW_PSCALEADJ.
The contract now states the `put b' fact so no future row rests on it.

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

0218:       rows paired with a liveness observable, because exit status cannot tell

0406: ## THE ENCLOSING CODE (the helper by brace count; the arms whose rows are new)
0407: 
0408: ```c
0409: static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
0410: {
0411: 	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
0412: 		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
0413: 		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
0414: 		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
0415: 	}
0416: }
0417: 
0418: 	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
0419: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
0420: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
0421: 			break;
0422: 		}
0423: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
0424: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
0425: 			if (idata & ~0xf0)
0426: 				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
0427: 				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
0428: 		}
0429: 		break;
0430: 
0431: 	case PCCTWO_T1CMP:
0432: 	case PCCTWO_T2CMP:
0433: 	case PCCTWO_T1COUNT:
0434: 	case PCCTWO_T2COUNT:
0435: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
0436: 		    relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
0437: 			memcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);
0438: 		break;
0439: 
0440: 	case PCCTWO_IPL:
0441: 		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
0442: 		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
0443: 		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above -- that is

1367: concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
1368: log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone

0389:     * per-instance vs process-global latch state: mvme187 instantiates ONE pcc2, so no
0390:       session can hold two instances.  The bitfield is per-instance by construction;

0393:       reassert_interrupts().  Every GENCTL lane value here keeps bit 0x04 CLEAR so no
0394:       row dies for that reason;

0396:       host in `default:';

0398:     * what real hardware does with the LANES of a wide write (not on disk);
0399:     * the SCCERR word-write's stored value: the arm always stores 0, so a decline and
0400:       an accept are value-identical there and the row's channel is the message.

0406: ## THE ENCLOSING CODE (the helper by brace count; the arms whose rows are new)
0407: 
0408: ```c
0409: static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
0410: {
0411: 	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
0412: 		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
0413: 		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
0414: 		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
0415: 	}
0416: }
0417: 
0418: 	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
0419: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
0420: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
0421: 			break;
0422: 		}
0423: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
0424: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
0425: 			if (idata & ~0xf0)
0426: 				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
0427: 				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
0428: 		}
0429: 		break;
0430:

0234:     return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)

0237: #  r2 = PCC2 base, r3 = scratch, r4 = load target, r5 = store source, r6 = second source
0238: #  ld.b / ld.h (0x07 / 0x06) SIGN-EXTEND: 0x82 would print as 0xffffff82.  Every row that
0239: #  asserts a VALUE uses the unsigned forms ld.bu / ld.hu (0x03 / 0x02); the survival rows
0240: #  only check exit status and keep the signed forms.
0241: def LD_B(rd, off): return enc(0x07, rd, 2, off)
0242: def LD_H(rd, off): return enc(0x06, rd, 2, off)

0244: def LD_HU(rd, off): return enc(0x02, rd, 2, off)

1363:## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

VERDICT: NO-GO

The claimed class closure is false. I found an analytically complete 118/118 escape within the fix-introduced `d->warned` state.

### 1. Enumeration and domain

I checked this denominator:

- 108 declared cells: 12 blocks × `{DEL, LEN, LEN2, KILL, ALIAS, FALL, SWAP, GROW, RDDECL}`.
- The two T1 shared-label paths.
- Helper TEST and SET operations.
- Widths `{1,2,4,8}` at all 12 blocks.
- FAULT at all 12 blocks.
- Latch-qualified guards at all 12 blocks.
- All 12 second-predicate dispositions.

The gate contains 118 unique row names, and both frozen BASE artifacts report 118/118. That zero is real but insufficient.

The S cells hold for the defined mutants. Re-derived from [dev_pcc2.c](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_pcc2.c:444), the landed `0xaab8` high-address lane is `0xb8`, producing:

- GENCTL/MASK `0xb8`
- VECBASE `0xb0`
- TxCTL `0x00`
- TxICR `0x10`
- SCCICR/SCCTX `0x18`
- SCCRX/SCSIICR `0x98`
- SCCERR `0x00`

These distinguish the recorded pre-values everywhere except SCCERR. PSCALEADJ and IPL instead expose landing through their body prints. DEL/LEN therefore share the claimed channels, TxICR/SCCTX FALL really shares their landed-write channel, and KILL shares the survival/liveness channel.

The E and UNREACHABLE warrants also hold:

- SCCERR is initialized to zero and every model writer leaves it zero.
- `|=`→`^=` or `+=` is equivalent while the TEST guarantees that bit is clear.
- A consistent injective bit translation is equivalent because no external code consumes `warned` bit identities.
- The six odd-only blocks’ wide-read cells are unreachable: the m88k alignment check precedes `memory_rw`, while debugger reads do not dispatch to this DM_DEFAULT device.

The C/W/A rows are sound for their narrowly defined, single-cell mutants. On mvme187, the other devices containing the generic decline suffix are not instantiated, so there is no identified benign inflation. But cardinality does not prove independence between latch bits.

#### Undisclosed 118/118 escape

At GENCTL, append:

```c
&& !(d->warned & 2u)
```

Bit 1 is VECBASE. This makes GENCTL decline only while VECBASE’s latch bit is clear.

All 118 rows remain green:

- Single-arm GENCTL sessions start with bit 1 clear.
- `rb2 genctl` sets only GENCTL’s own bit, so both writes remain declined.
- `latch all`, `latch all w`, and `latch all d` visit GENCTL before VECBASE and never revisit GENCTL.
- In `latch aba`, the first GENCTL announces, VECBASE announces, and the final GENCTL write lands. The row still counts exactly two announcements and performs no final readback. Thus `latch aba` stays green while GENCTL becomes `0x03`.

This is fix-introduced state, not an arbitrary pre-existing device field. “Own latch bit set” is closed; “another latch bit set” is not.

There is also an uncovered width/state cross-product. A guard qualified so that only a second `len==8` write lands passes because `rb2 *` uses only halfwords, while `latch all d` performs only one dword per block. The detector covers all widths with a clear latch and length 2 with a set latch, not `{2,4,8} × {clear,set}`.

State walk from the live source:

- `pcctwo_reg[]` is given non-initial target values at the nine storing channels, but unrelated register state and active IPL state are not covered.
- `pending_timer1_interrupts` and `pending_timer2_interrupts` start at zero; no detector row constructs enabled timer + compare crossing + enabled ICR to make either nonzero.
- `cur_int_vec[]` starts at zero and no row deliberately creates the interrupt state that writes it.
- `d->warned` now exercises each own bit set, but not arbitrary other-bit sets as guard inputs.

Therefore the domain must either explicitly exclude pre-existing timer/interrupt state under a defined mutation grammar, or add non-initial-state sessions. It cannot presently claim the whole decision state.

### 2. Smallest passing edit

If production announcement severity counts as part of the property, the absolute smallest escape remains the disclosed one-token `fatal`→`debug`; all rows run with `-V`, so all 118 stay green although normal quiet operation can lose the announcement.

The smallest important undisclosed class escape is the cross-bit GENCTL qualifier above. The specifically misleading green row is `latch aba`; analytically, the complete result remains 118/118.

Minimum repair:

- Prime all twelve latch bits, then exercise every block again through its readback or print-body channel; SCCERR remains E.
- Repeat the set-state effect check for widths 4 and 8, not just 2.
- Measure the cross-bit mutant explicitly.
- Either bound the mutation grammar against `pending_timer*`, `cur_int_vec[]`, and unrelated registers, or add representative active timer/interrupt-state rows.

### 3. Option-set completeness

N/A is correct: no disposition choice is being made.

### 4. Record honesty

Several corrections are required:

- `PRETABLE_pass5.out` predicts `M_QUAL_GENCTL` produces `rb2 genctl = 0xbb`; the actual artifact reports `0xb8`, matching the row’s `0xaab8` input. That sentence is measurement-refuted.
- “Six mutants” names BASE plus five unique mutants. It should say six builds/configurations or five mutants plus BASE.
- “Every write arm uses `idata`” is false: SCCERR stores constant zero, and IPL does not use `idata`.
- The opening still says “this diff adds five rows” and later asks for an escape passing 96 rows. The delivered detector is 101→118, with 17 new rows in this pass.
- [PRETABLE_pass4.out](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/pcc2widthkills/killtable2_20260903/PRETABLE_pass4.out:1) says its mtime witnesses precedence over part 4, but its mtime is `01:23:26Z`, after part 4’s `driver.log` at `00:10:26Z`. The main PRETABLE candidly disclaims its own mtime, but the pass-4 file’s claim is not honest as currently stored.
- The packet reports `CHANGELOG.md` as `356/0`; the live diff is `361/0`. The changelog and gate log changed 41 seconds after the brief was frozen, so this is a post-packet mutation rather than proof the frozen count was originally fabricated. The disclosure nevertheless no longer describes the live tree and must be refreshed or labeled as a frozen snapshot.
- The packet does not provide a complete pass-5/pass-6 roster, so the stated closing-roster condition cannot be independently settled from the packet.

I re-derived the guard census, enum cardinality, arm arithmetic, state initialization/writers, SCCERR equivalence, reassert call count, reachability, and helper-consumer claims from the live `b78bb0f` fork. Measurement results and chronology came from the stored artifacts only; I ran no gate.
